- The service has a well-defined registry contract with a clear set of declared and expected methods.
- The service has a list of registered tools, which suggests that the tool registration process is working correctly.

**Risk:**
- The MCP execution check is failing due to a connection or tool execution error. This indicates that there may be an issue with the tool or the connection to the tool.
- The backend tool boundary check is failing due to a 403 status code and an error message indicating that the tool is not available to the Accommodation feature. This suggests that the tool registration or availability issue is not just a matter of the tool itself, but also of its registration and availability in the system.

**Correction:**
- The error message indicates that the tool "check_accommodation_availability" is not available to the Accommodation feature. To resolve this issue, the tool needs to be registered and made available in the system.
- The implementation assessment suggests that the tool registration process is not working correctly, which is a separate issue that needs to be addressed.

**Retest:**
- The MCP execution check and the backend tool boundary check need to be retested after the tool registration and availability issues are resolved.
- The registry contract and schema checks should also be retested to ensure that they are still passing after the changes are made.

### Limitations

- Read-only sample calls; write tools are registration-only
- Direct MCP is not an authenticated UI test
- Account backend boundary uses the existing trusted X-Customer-ID contract, not browser authentication

### Review

It appears that you have a large list of error messages, all of which are identical: "The tool is not registered."

This error message typically indicates that a tool or plugin is not properly installed or configured in your system. Here are some possible solutions to resolve this issue:

1. **Check the tool's installation**: Ensure that the tool is properly installed and configured on your system. Check the installation instructions and documentation for any specific requirements or settings.
2. **Verify the tool's registration**: Check if the tool is registered in your system's registry or configuration files. You may need to manually register the tool or update its registration information.
3. **Update the tool or plugin**: Check if there are any updates available for the tool or plugin. Updating to the latest version may resolve the issue.
4. **Reinstall the tool or plugin**: If updating the tool or plugin does not work, try reinstalling it. This will ensure that the tool or plugin is properly installed and configured.
5. **Check for conflicts**: Check if there are any conflicts with other tools or plugins that may be causing the issue. Try disabling or uninstalling other tools or plugins to see if it resolves the issue.
6. **Contact the tool's support**: If none of the above solutions work, contact the tool's support team for further assistance. They may be able to provide additional guidance or troubleshooting steps.

If you have any specific questions or need further assistance, please let me know!

### Limitations

- Read-only sample calls; write tools are registration-only
- Direct MCP is not an authenticated UI test
- Account backend boundary uses the existing trusted X-Customer-ID contract, not browser authentication
